Whose daughter had to be sacrificed to Artemis in order for the Greeks to obtain favorable winds to sail to Troy? ANSWER:AGAMEMNON’S / CLYTEMNESTRA’S  BONUS: At what port city did this sacrifice take place? ANSWER:AULIS  BONUS: Who was this unfortunate maiden? ANSWER:IPHIGENEIA  
Whom did King Alcinous and Queen Arete help after hearing of his disastrous attempts to return home? ANSWER:ODYSSEUS  BONUS: What daughter of Alcinous and Arete was the princess of the Phaeacians? ANSWER:NAUSICAA  BONUS: What was the name of the island over which Alcinous ruled? ANSWER:SCHERIA  
Name the two Atridae. ANSWER:AGAMEMNON AND MENELAUS  BONUS: Why were Agamemnon and Menelaus referred to by this name? ANSWER:THEIR FATHER WAS ATREUS / THEY ARE THE SONS OF ATREUS  BONUS: What twin giants, eventually killed by Apollo, were the sons of Aloeus and thus known as the Aloidae? ANSWER:OTUS AND EPHIALTES  
Whose fourteen children were killed by Apollo and Artemis? ANSWER:NIOBE’S  BONUS: Into what did Niobe turn because of grief for her stricken children? ANSWER:A (WEEPING) ROCK  BONUS: Why did Apollo and Artemis kill the children of Niobe? ANSWER: SHE HAD BRAGGED THAT SHE WAS SUPERIOR TO LETO DUE TO HER GREATER AMOUNT OF CHILLUNS / CHICKLETS  
What Ithacan is usually said to be Odysseus’ father? ANSWER:LAERTES  BONUS: Who is the mother of Odysseus? ANSWER:ANTICLEA  BONUS: Some stories, however, say that what cunning mythological thief seduced Anticlea and was the actual father of Odysseus? ANSWER:AUTOLYCUS  
What son of Autonoe was torn apart by his own hounds as punishment for seeing Artemis as she bathed nude? ANSWER:ACTAEON  BONUS: Who were the parents of Autonoe, as well as Agave, Ino, Semele, and Polydorus? ANSWER:CADMUS AND HARMONIA  BONUS: Who were the parents of Harmonia? ANSWER:ARES AND APHRODITE  
Who had once been a beautiful girl, but was made hideous with hair of serpents and a truly stony glare by an enraged Athena? ANSWER:MEDUSA  BONUS: Why was Athena so angry at Medusa? ANSWER: MEDUSA AND POSEIDON WERE INTIMATE IN ATHENA’S TEMPLE  BONUS: What two other immortal Gorgons did Medusa join? ANSWER: STHENO AND EURYALE  
What queen’s last moments were presided over by Iris, who cut a lock of hair after the queen committed suicide while watching the Trojan ships sail away from Carthage? ANSWER:DIDO’S  BONUS: What sister of Dido was distraught to discover that Dido had killed herself? ANSWER:ANNA  BONUS: What African chieftain had wanted to marry Dido, but had been rejected? ANSWER:IARBAS  
What king of Troy was famous for never keeping his word? ANSWER:LAOMEDON  BONUS: What two gods helped Laomedon to build Troy's walls, but went unpaid by the king? ANSWER:APOLLO AND POSEIDON/NEPTUNE  BONUS: What was one of the punishments which Apollo and Poseidon sent for this breach of contract? ANSWER:PLAGUE, SEA-MONSTER  
What villain, sometimes known as Polypemon or Damastes, was thwarted by Theseus, who stretched him upon his own bed? ANSWER:PROCRUSTES  BONUS: What villain, also bested by Theseus, kicked people over a cliff to their death? ANSWER:SCIRON  BONUS: Sciron's victims did not always die by landing on the rocks below. What other danger lurked in the waters below? ANSWER:(MAN-EATING SEA) TURTLE  
What giant guarded the island of Crete? ANSWER:TALUS  BONUS: Of what material was Talus made? ANSWER:BRONZE  BONUS: Who drained the life-sustaining ichor from Talus' body? ANSWER:MEDEA  
What was physically unusual about Zetes and Calais? ANSWER: THEY HAD WINGS / COULD FLY  BONUS: What wind carried off the nymph Oreithyia, on whom he sired the twins Zetes and Calais? ANSWER:BOREAS  BONUS: What other wind was responsible for the death of Hyacinthus? ANSWER:ZEPHYR(US)  
What woman was placed in the heavens on a chair, but sitting upside-down as further punishment for the pride that had almost cost her the life of her daughter Andromeda? ANSWER:CASSIOPEIA  BONUS: What god had sent the monster to kill Andromeda and had turned Cassiopeia into a constellation? ANSWER:POSEIDON / NEPTUNE  BONUS: Who was the husband of Cassiopeia? ANSWER:CEPHEUS  
Who was the daughter of the Aeolian king Thestius, the wife of Tyndareus, and the mother of two children by Zeus, who came to her as a swan? ANSWER:LEDA  BONUS: Name the two children of Zeus and Leda. ANSWER: POLLUX/POLYDEUCES AND HELEN  BONUS: Helen, being divinely beautiful, had a correspondingly godly number of suitors. What oath did all of these suitors have to take? ANSWER: TO SUPPORT HER FUTURE HUSBAND / TO COME TO HER AID IF SHE WERE EVER IN TROUBLE  
What Thracian king was fed his own son for dinner after he raped and mutilated his wife’s sister? ANSWER:TEREUS  BONUS: Who was this sister, an Athenian princess who eventually was transformed into a nightingale? ANSWER:PHILOMELA  BONUS: What specific act of mutilation did Tereus perform upon Philomela? ANSWER:DETONGUED HER  
What ability did Periclymenus, Vertumnus, Proteus, and Achelous have in common? ANSWER:SHAPE-SHIFTING (OR SIMILAR)  BONUS: In the Odyssey, how did Menelaus and three companions disguise themselves in  order to trick Proteus into falling asleep in front of them so they could bind him? ANSWER:AS SEALS  BONUS: Why did Menelaus want to talk to Proteus so much? ANSWER: (PROTEUS’ DAUGHTER EIDOTHEA TOLD HIM THAT) PROTEUS KNEW WHAT MENELAUS HAD TO DO TO GET HOME (TO SPARTA) / TO FIND A WAY OUT OF THE DESERT  
What priests of Mars received their name from ritual dancing? ANSWER:SALIĪ BONUS: What was the Latin term for the sacred item, protected by the Saliī, that supposedly fell from the sky during the reign of Numa? ANSWER:ANCĪLE / ANCĪLIA BONUS: What other group of Saliī were dedicated to Quirinus? ANSWER:SALIĪ COLLĪNĪ  
A boat race, a footrace, and a wrestling match all figured in whose funeral games, held in Sicily by Aeneas and his men? ANSWER:ANCHISES  BONUS: What king of Eryx in Sicily had extended hospitality to the Trojans? ANSWER:ACESTES  BONUS: Despite burying his father, Aeneas was able to speak to him again in the Underworld. What woman guided Aeneas through the Underworld? ANSWER: (CUMAEAN) SIBYL / DEIPHOBE  
The animosity between what divinity and what hero resulted in the formation of the Milky Way? ANSWER:HERA and HERACLES  BONUS: What city was the center of the worship of Hera on the Greek mainland? ANSWER:ARGOS  BONUS: What island was the center of Hera's worship in the Aegean? ANSWER:SAMOS  
What king was warned by the Delphic oracle that Aeolus’ descendant, a man with one sandal, would one day cause his death? ANSWER:PELIAS  BONUS: What god’s annual festival was chosen by Jason as the occasion at which he would declare his right to the throne of Iolcus? ANSWER:POSEIDON’S  BONUS: Where had Jason been until his twenty-first year? ANSWER: MT. PELION / MAGNESIA (PROMPT ON “WITH CHIRON”)  
Whose death in Book XVI of Homer’s Iliad precipitated Achilles’ return to the fighting after his withdrawal from combat following his bitter dispute with Agamemnon? ANSWER:PATROCLUS BONUS:Over what slave-girl’s ownership had Achilles and Agamemnon fought? ANSWER:BRISEIS’ BONUS:Besides cremating his body on a pyre, what did Achilles do in Book XXIII of the Iliad to commemorate his fallen friend? ANSWER:HOSTED FUNERAL GAMES (ATHLETIC CONTESTS) 
Which of the major deities of the Greek pantheon was the only one with a mortal mother, though he was, in the end, actually born from Zeus’ thigh? ANSWER:DIONYSUS/BACCHUS BONUS: Which of the major deities of the Greek pantheon was, in some versions, born from Hera alone? ANSWER:HEPHAESTUS BONUS: Name one of the two minor goddesses who were also children of Hera. ANSWER:HEBE OR EILEITHYIA 
What hero was responsible for the deaths of Phaea, Damastes, Corynetes, Sinis and, somewhat indirectly, his own father Aegeus? ANSWER:THESEUS BONUS:What did Theseus fail to do that caused Aegeus to throw himself off of a cliff into the sea? ANSWER:CHANGE THE (BLACK) SAILS (OF HIS SHIP) TO WHITE (ON THE WAY BACK TO ATHENS FROM CRETE AFTER SLAYING THE MINOTAUR) BONUS:What young girl of Sparta, world-renowned for her beauty, did Theseus kidnap during his later years as a sort of wife-in-waiting for himself? ANSWER:HELEN 
What youth’s beauty earned him the attention of both Zephyrus and Apollo? ANSWER:HYACINTH(US) BONUS:Into what kind of tree did Apollo transform his lover Cyparissus when he proved inconsolable after the death of a beloved pet stag? ANSWER:CYPRESS BONUS:Whose infidelity caused Apollo to change the feathers of the crow from white to black? ANSWER:CORONIS’ 
For what Boeotian city did the sons of Zeus and Antiope, Amphion and Zethus, construct the walls? ANSWER:THEBES BONUS:What special ability did Amphion utilize to complete this task? ANSWER:MUSIC (THE STONES MOVED ON THEIR OWN IN RESPONSE TO HIS LYRE-PLAYING) BONUS:What was Zethus’ special connection to the name of the city? ANSWER:IT WAS NEWLY RENAMED AFTER HIS WIFE (THEBE) 
What goddess was the patron goddess of marriage and child birth? ANSWER:JUNO/HERA BONUS: What hero’s birth did Juno try to prevent by refusing to allow his mother to deliver him? ANSWER:HERCULES (DO NOT ACCEPT HERACLES) BONUS: What twins’ birth did Hera try to prevent by declaring that their mother could not give birth on terra firma? APOLLO AND ARTEMIS (DO NOT ACCEPT DIANA) ANSWER: and LIFE 
What personification of the sky was the principal partner of Gaia and the father of the Titans? ANSWER:(O)URANUS BONUS:What goddess, according to Hesiod, rose from the foam of the sea that formed after Cronus severed Uranos’ genitals with a sickle? ANSWER:APHRODITE BONUS:What final monstrous and nearly indescribable child did Gaia produce in order to punish Zeus after he imprisoned the Titans in the Underworld? ANSWER:TYPH(A)ON/TYPHOEUS 
Who was the mortal half-brother of Heracles who cried in the face of snakes invading his crib? ANSWER:IPHICLES BONUS: Name Heracles’ nephew and charioteer who provided him invaluable assistance on many occasions, particularly against Lernaean Hydra when he quickly cauterized the stumps of the necks as Heracles severed the snake’s heads. ANSWER:IOLAUS BONUS:Who was Heracles’ son by Deianira, his last wife? ANSWER: HYLLUS 
Whose shade did Circe direct Odysseus to summon from the Underworld and consult before he journeyed home to Ithaca? ANSWER:TIRESIAS’ BONUS:Before speaking to Tiresias, what close relative’s shade did Odysseus also encounter—much to his sorrowful surprise—and attempt to embrace? ANSWER:ANTICLEA’S (PROMPT ON “HIS MOTHER’S”) BONUS:What monster with six dog-heads, encountered by Odysseus on his way home, had once been a beautiful woman but was transformed by Circe out of jealousy? ANSWER:SCYLLA 
Of the characters Dido, Anchises, Creusa, Turnus, and Camilla, who dies last in Vergil’s Aeneid? ANSWER:TURNUS BONUS: What Italian princess had Turnus originally planned to marry before her father betrothed her to Aeneas? ANSWER:LAVINIA BONUS:Complete the following analogy: Achilles : Patroclus :: Aeneas : _______. ANSWER:PALLAS 
What two brothers bitterly connived and conspired against each other for the throne of Mycenae, engaging in a feud that culminated in one tricking the other to dine upon the flesh of his own sons? ANSWER:ATREUS and THYESTES BONUS:What ivory-shouldered man who had once been served as a meal himself was the father of Atreus and Thyestes? ANSWER:PELOPS BONUS:What mournful mother of fourteen children that were struck down by the arrows of Apollo and Artemis was Pelops’ sister? ANSWER:NIOBE 
Many mythological stories involve trees. What type of tree had is fruit dyed red by the blood of the lovers Pyramus and Thisbe? ANSWER:MULBERRY TREE BONUS: What couple was turned into two trees with a conjoined trunk? ANSWER:BAUCIS AND PHILEMON BONUS: Into what trees were they each turned? OAK AND LINDEN ANSWER: and LIFE 
Whose hand in marriage did Cepheus and Cassiopeia consent to give to Perseus before the hero saved her from being sacrificed to a sea monster? ANSWER:ANDROMEDA’S BONUS:What group of goddesses had Cassiopeia offended with her boasts about her own and Andromeda’s beauty? ANSWER:NEREIDS BONUS:To whom had Andromeda been previously engaged before the arrival of Perseus? ANSWER:HER UNCLE / PHINEUS 
What Greek hero slew the Ethiopian prince Memnon near the end of the Trojan War? ANSWER:ACHILLES BONUS:What Greek goddess of the dawn was the mother of Memnon? ANSWER:EOS BONUS:What Amazonian princess did Achilles slay but afterwards lament? ANSWER:PENTHESILEA 
Who, when the goddess discovered that she was pregnant, was punished by Diana by being transformed into a bear? ANSWER:ARCAS BONUS:Whose guise, according to Ovid, had Jupiter assumed to snare the otherwise virginal Callisto in his embrace? ANSWER:DIANA’S 
Whose fear of a one-sandaled man proved well founded when a young man fitting the description arrived and laid claim to the throne of Iolcus? ANSWER:PELIAS’ BONUS:Who had been the rightful king of Iolcus before Pelias usurped the throne? ANSWER:AESON BONUS:Who brought about Pelias’ death by tricking his daughters into cutting him into pieces and throwing them into a boiling pot with the false expectation of his magical rejuvenation? ANSWER:MEDEA 
Which deity was the first to be born from Rhea and the last to be regurgitated from Cronus and therefore, according to the Homeric Hymns, the first and last to be honored with the offering of wine at the banquets of men? ANSWER:HESTIA BONUS:Which god was sometimes said to have escaped Cronus’ maw when he was switched out for a foal instead? ANSWER:POSEIDON BONUS:Who were the Roman equivalents of Cronus and Rhea? ANSWER:SATURN and OPS

Which character from Book III of Ovid’s Metamorphoses speaks the following lines? “I love him and I cannot seem to find him! To make it worse, no sea, no road, no mountain, No city-wall, no gate, no barrier, parts us But a thin film of water. He is eager For me to hold him. When my lips go down To kiss the pool, his rise, he reaches toward me. “ ANSWER:NARCISSUS BONUS:Earlier in Ovid’s account, a young man rejected by Narcissus prays that the beautiful youth would one day also be unable to win over the person whom he loves. What goddess of vengeance heard this prayer? ANSWER:NEMESIS BONUS:When Narcissus’ mother Liriope asked Tiresias if her son would live to a ripe old age, what was the seer’s cryptic reply? ANSWER:“YES, IF HE NEVER KNOWS HIMSELF” (OR EQUIVALENT) 
What deity had granted Theseus three curses, of which one was used by the hero to bring about the death of his son Hippolytus? ANSWER:POSEIDON BONUS:Which deities did Hippolytus scorn and cherish, RESPECTIVELY, according to a tragedy written by Euripides? ANSWER:APHRODITE and ARTEMIS BONUS:Why did Theseus wish Hippolytus dead? ANSWER:HE (MISTAKENLY) BELIEVED THAT HIPPOLYTUS HAD ATTEMPTED TO RAPE PHAEDRA (PROMPT ON “THESEUS’ WIFE”) 
Who slept overnight at Athena’s altar upon the advice of the seer Polyeidus and awoke the next morning to find a magical, golden bridle with which he could tame the winged Pegasus? ANSWER:BELLEROPHON BONUS:Bellerophon rode Pegasus to defeat the fire-breathing Chimaera. Which three constituent animals make up the Chimaera’s monstrous body? ANSWER:GOAT, LION, SNAKE BONUS:What kind of creature did Zeus send to disrupt Bellerophon’s ill-conceived attempt to fly Pegasus up to Olympus? ANSWER:GADFLY / BRIZE

ANSWER: KILLED AND SERVED THYESTES’ SONS TO HIM AS A MEAL (N.B. key components of answer: Atreus killing + Thyestes eating)
